   * Returns the perceived brightness of the color, from 0-255.

  }
  /**
   * Returns the perceived luminance of a color, from 0-1.

  }

  }

   */
  toHex8(t = !1) {
    return hg(this.r, this.g, this.b, this.a, t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the hex 8 value of the color -with a # prefixed.
   * @param allow4Char will shorten hex value to 4 char if possible
   */
  toHex8String(t = !1) {
    return "#" + this.toHex8(t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the shorter hex value of the color depends on its alpha -with a # prefixed.
   * @param allowShortChar will shorten hex value to 3 or 4 char if possible
   */
  toHexShortString(t = !1) {
    return this.a === 1 ? this.toHexString(t) : this.toHex8String(t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the object as a RGBA object.
   */
  toRgb() {
    return {
      r: Math.round(this.r),
      g: Math.round(this.g),
      b: Math.round(this.b),
      a: this.a
    };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the RGBA values interpolated into a string with the following format:
   * "RGBA(xxx, xxx, xxx, xx)".
   */
  toRgbString() {
    const t = Math.round(this.r), n = Math.round(this.g), l = Math.round(this.b);
    return this.a === 1 ? `rgb(${t}, ${n}, ${l})` : `rgba(${t}, ${n}, ${l}, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the object as a RGBA object.
   */
  toPercentageRgb() {
    const t = (n) => `${Math.round(bt(n, 255) * 100)}%`;
    return {
      r: t(this.r),
      g: t(this.g),
      b: t(this.b),
      a: this.a
    };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the RGBA relative values interpolated into a string
   */
  toPercentageRgbString() {
    const t = (n) => Math.round(bt(n, 255) * 100);
    return this.a === 1 ? `rgb(${t(this.r)}%, ${t(this.g)}%, ${t(this.b)}%)` : `rgba(${t(this.r)}%, ${t(this.g)}%, ${t(this.b)}%, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  toCmyk() {
    return {
      ...Ls(this.r, this.g, this.b)
    };
  }
  toCmykString() {
    const { c: t, m: n, y: l, k: i } = Ls(this.r, this.g, this.b);
    return `cmyk(${t}, ${n}, ${l}, ${i})`;
  }
  /**
   * The 'real' name of the color -if there is one.
   */
  toName() {
    if (this.a === 0)
      return "transparent";
    if (this.a < 1)
      return !1;
    const t = "#" + Os(this.r, this.g, this.b, !1);
    for (const [n, l] of Object.entries(Ha))
      if (t === l)
        return n;
    return !1;
  }
  toString(t) {
    const n = !!t;
    t = t ?? this.format;
    let l = !1;
    const i = this.a < 1 && this.a >= 0;
    return !n && i && (t.startsWith("hex") || t === "name") ? t === "name" && this.a === 0 ? this.toName() : this.toRgbString() : (t === "rgb" && (l = this.toRgbString()), t === "prgb" && (l = this.toPercentageRgbString()), (t === "hex" || t === "hex6") && (l = this.toHexString()), t === "hex3" && (l = this.toHexString(!0)), t === "hex4" && (l = this.toHex8String(!0)), t === "hex8" && (l = this.toHex8String()), t === "name" && (l = this.toName()), t === "hsl" && (l = this.toHslString()), t === "hsv" && (l = this.toHsvString()), t === "cmyk" && (l = this.toCmykString()), l || this.toHexString());
  }
  toNumber() {
    return (Math.round(this.r) << 16) + (Math.round(this.g) << 8) + Math.round(this.b);
  }
  clone() {
    return new Qe(this.toString());
  }
  /**
   * Lighten the color a given amount. Providing 100 will always return white.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  lighten(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.l += t / 100, n.l = _l(n.l), new Qe(n);
  }
  /**
   * Brighten the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  brighten(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toRgb();
    return n.r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n.r - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), n.g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n.g - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), n.b = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n.b - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), new Qe(n);
  }
  /**
   * Darken the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 100 will always return black.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  darken(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.l -= t / 100, n.l = _l(n.l), new Qe(n);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the color with pure white, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 0 will do nothing, providing 100 will always return white.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  tint(t = 10) {
    return this.mix("white", t);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the color with pure black, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 0 will do nothing, providing 100 will always return black.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  shade(t = 10) {
    return this.mix("black", t);
  }
  /**
   * Desaturate the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 100 will is the same as calling greyscale
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  desaturate(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.s -= t / 100, n.s = _l(n.s), new Qe(n);
  }
  /**
   * Saturate the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  saturate(t = 10) {
    const n = this.toHsl();
    return n.s += t / 100, n.s = _l(n.s), new Qe(n);
  }
  /**
   * Completely desaturates a color into greyscale.
   * Same as calling `desaturate(100)`
   */
  greyscale() {
    return this.desaturate(100);
  }
  /**
   * Spin takes a positive or negative amount within [-360, 360] indicating the change of hue.
   * Values outside of this range will be wrapped into this range.
   */
  spin(t) {
    const n = this.toHsl(), l = (n.h + t) % 360;
    return n.h = l < 0 ? 360 + l : l, new Qe(n);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the current color a given amount with another color, from 0 to 100.
   * 0 means no mixing (return current color).
   */
  mix(t, n = 50) {
    const l = this.toRgb(), i = new Qe(t).toRgb(), o = n / 100, s = {
      r: (i.r - l.r) * o + l.r,
      g: (i.g - l.g) * o + l.g,
      b: (i.b - l.b) * o + l.b,
      a: (i.a - l.a) * o + l.a
    };
    return new Qe(s);
  }
  analogous(t = 6, n = 30) {
    const l = this.toHsl(), i = 360 / n, o = [this];
    for (l.h = (l.h - (i * t >> 1) + 720) % 360; --t; )
      l.h = (l.h + i) % 360, o.push(new Qe(l));
    return o;
  }
  /**
   * taken from https://github.com/infusion/jQuery-xcolor/blob/master/jquery.xcolor.js
   */
  complement() {
    const t = this.toHsl();
    return t.h = (t.h + 180) % 360, new Qe(t);
  }
  monochromatic(t = 6) {
    const n = this.toHsv(), { h: l } = n, { s: i } = n;
    let { v: o } = n;
    const s = [], r = 1 / t;
    for (; t--; )
      s.push(new Qe({ h: l, s: i, v: o })), o = (o + r) % 1;
    return s;
  }
  splitcomplement() {
    const t = this.toHsl(), { h: n } = t;
    return [
      this,
      new Qe({ h: (n + 72) % 360, s: t.s, l: t.l }),
      new Qe({ h: (n + 216) % 360, s: t.s, l: t.l })
    ];
  }
  /**
   * Compute how the color would appear on a background
   */
  onBackground(t) {
    const n = this.toRgb(), l = new Qe(t).toRgb(), i = n.a + l.a * (1 - n.a);
    return new Qe({
      r: (n.r * n.a + l.r * l.a * (1 - n.a)) / i,
      g: (n.g * n.a + l.g * l.a * (1 - n.a)) / i,
      b: (n.b * n.a + l.b * l.a * (1 - n.a)) / i,
      a: i
    });
  }
  /**
   * Alias for `polyad(3)`
   */
  triad() {
    return this.polyad(3);
  }
  /**
   * Alias for `polyad(4)`
   */
  tetrad() {
    return this.polyad(4);
  }
  /**
   * Get polyad colors, like (for 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, etc...)
   * monad, dyad, triad, tetrad, pentad, hexad, heptad, octad, etc...
   */
  polyad(t) {
    const n = this.toHsl(), { h: l } = n, i = [this], o = 360 / t;
    for (let s = 1; s < t; s++)
      i.push(new Qe({ h: (l + s * o) % 360, s: n.s, l: n.l }));
    return i;
  }
  /**
   * compare color vs current color
   */
  equals(t) {
    const n = new Qe(t);
    return this.format === "cmyk" || n.format === "cmyk" ? this.toCmykString() === n.toCmykString() : this.toRgbString() === n.toRgbString();
  }
}
function qt(e, t = 20) {
  return e.mix("#141414", t).toString();
}

function Sp(e, t) {
  const n = e.width * e.tileSize, l = e.height * e.tileSize, i = Math.min(t.w / n, t.h / l), o = n * i, s = l * i, r = t.x + (t.w - o) / 2, c = t.y + (t.h - s) / 2;
  return Object.freeze({
    scale: i,
    rect: Object.freeze({ x: r, y: c, w: o, h: s }),
    worldToDisplay(d, u) {
      return [r + d * i, c + u * i];
    },
    displayToWorld(d, u) {
      return d < r || u < c || d >= r + o || u >= c + s ? null : [(d - r) / i, (u - c) / i];
    },
    tileCenterToDisplay(d, u, $ = 0, O = 0) {
      return this.worldToDisplay((d - $) * 16 + 8, (u - O) * 16 + 8);
    }
  });
}

function _C({ root: e, views: t, navigate: n, logout: l, accounts: i, draft: o, stageAssets: s, stageJobs: r, library: c, stronghold: d, mapview: u, catalog: $, generals: O, copy: A, fullCopy: S, publication: E, management: y, review: b, deletion: C }) {

games: [], lines: [], locked: !0 }), me = /* @__PURE__ */ _e({ categories: [], category: "portrait", filter: "", rows: [], total: 0, loaded: !1, message: "", loading: !1 }), K = /* @__PURE__ */ _e({ games: [], chapters: [], generals: [], factions: [], library: [], locked: !0, saveDisabled: !0 }), Z = /* @__PURE__ */ _e({ name: "", introduction: "", games: [], locked: !0, createDisabled: !0, tracking: !1, message: "" }), we = /* @__PURE__ */ _e({ visible: !1, name: "", introduction: "", message: "", locked: !0, createDisabled: !0, tracking: !1 }), Ee = /* @__PURE__ */ _e({ rows: [], locked: !0, refreshDisabled: !0, queryDisabled: !0, retryDisabled: !0, forgetDisabled: !0, operation: "", result: "", message: "" }), de = /* @__PURE__ */ _e({ games: [], locked: !0, queryDisabled: } }), ve = /* @__PURE__ */ _e({ games: [], admin: !1, locked: !0, queryDisabled: !0, retryDisabled: !0, forgetDisabled: !0 }), Be = Jf(JC, { state: x, accountsModel: h, accounts: i, draftModel: v,   return Object.freeze({
    accounts: (ye) => Object.assign(h, ye),
    draft: (ye) => Object.assign(v, ye),
    stageAssets: (ye) => Object.assign(M, ye),
    stageJobs: (ye) => Object.assign(q, ye),
    library: (ye) => Object.assign(se, ye),
    stronghold: (ye) => Object.assign(ee, ye),
    mapview: (ye) => Object.assign(oe, ye),
    catalog: (ye) => Object.assign(me, ye),
    generals: (ye) => Object.assign(K, ye),
    copy: (ye) => Object.assign(Z, ye),
    fullCopy: (ye) => Object.assign(we, ye),
    publication: (ye) => Object.assign(Ee, ye),

      Object.assign(x, {
